```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash

numbers=(42 17 93 8 55 71 3 88 29 64)
min=${numbers[0]}
max=${numbers[0]}
sum=0

for n in "${numbers[@]}"; do
  (( n < min )) && min=$n
  (( n > max )) && max=$n
  (( sum += n ))
done

printf 'min: %d\nmax: %d\nsum: %d\n' "$min" "$max" "$sum"
```

The script is saved at `/home/eval/sort_uploads.sh`. It processes regular files directly inside `/uploads`, puts files without an extension in `other/`, and prints move and count lines. Run it with:

```bash
/home/eval/sort_uploads.sh
```

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
set -euo pipefail

UPLOAD_DIR=/uploads
declare -A counts=()
shopt -s nullglob dotglob

for file in "$UPLOAD_DIR"/*; do
  [[ -f "$file" ]] || continue

  name=${file##*/}
  if [[ "$name" == *.* && "$name" != .* && "$name" != *. ]]; then
    ext=${name##*.}
  elif [[ "$name" == .*.* && "$name" != *. ]]; then
    ext=${name##*.}
  else
    ext=other
  fi

  dest="$UPLOAD_DIR/$ext"
  mkdir -p -- "$dest"
  mv -- "$file" "$dest/"
  printf 'moved: %s -> %s/\n' "$name" "$ext"
  counts["$ext"]=$(( ${counts["$ext"]:-0} + 1 ))
done

while IFS= read -r ext; do
  [[ -n "$ext" ]] && printf '%s: %d files\n' "$ext" "${counts[$ext]}"
done < <(printf '%s\n' "${!counts[@]}" | sort)
```
